\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\rangle\right]\leq-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{2\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]+G^{\delta}_{t}.

(52)

For term II in
48
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\delta_{t}z_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t+1}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\left(C_{\Theta}C_{\phi}^{2}B\beta_{t}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)

\displaystyle=(k+1)C^{3}_{\phi}BU_{\delta}C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}+\frac{2(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}},

(53)

where the last inequality is from
Lemma

2
and
Proposition

4
.

To convenience, we rewrite
F_{t}=F_{\omega_{t}}
. To bound term III in
48
, note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\rangle\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\theta^{*}_{t+1}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},F_{t}^{-1}\nabla J(\omega_{t})-F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla J(\omega_{t+1})\right\rangle\right]+\frac{\beta_{t}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2\beta_{t}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\beta_{t}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2\beta_{t}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta^{*}_{t+1}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},F_{t+1}^{-1}\left(\nabla J(\omega_{t})-\nabla J(\omega_{t+1})\right)\right\rangle\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\left(F_{t}^{-1}-F^{-1}_{t+1}\right)\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\left(\omega_{t}-\omega_{t+1}\right)\right\rangle\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\left(F_{t}^{-1}-F^{-1}_{t+1}\right)\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\left(\omega_{t}-\omega_{t+1}\right)\right\rangle\right]+\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|F_{t}-F_{t+1}\right\|_{2}\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}-\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\left(\phi_{t}^{\top}(s_{t},a_{t})\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)\right\rangle\right]+\frac{BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{-\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\phi_{t}(s,a)\right]\right\rangle\right]}_{(i)}

\displaystyle\qquad-\underbrace{\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)(\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t})\phi_{t}(s,a)\right]\right\rangle\right]}_{(ii)}

\displaystyle\qquad-\underbrace{\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})\phi_{t}(s,a)\right]\right\rangle\right]}_{(iii)}

\displaystyle\qquad-\underbrace{\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\left(\phi_{t}^{\top}(s_{t},a_{t})\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s,a)\right]\right)\right\rangle\right]}_{(iv)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)+2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}{2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}.

(54)

where
(a)
follows from
Proposition

4
,
(b)
follows from Lagrange’s Mean Value for some
\lambda\in[0,1]
, such that
\hat{\omega}_{t}=\lambda\omega_{t}+(1-\lambda)\omega_{t+1}
,
(c)

follows from the facts that for positive definite matrices
X
and
Y
,

\displaystyle\left\|X^{-1}-Y^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|X^{-1}\left(X-Y\right)Y^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|X^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|X-Y\right\|_{2}\left\|Y^{-1}\right\|_{2},

(55)

and
(d)
is from that

\displaystyle\left\|F_{t+1}-F_{t}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{t+1}}\left[\phi_{t+1}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t+1}(s,a)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t}(s,a)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{t+1}}\left[\phi_{t+1}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t+1}(s,a)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t+1}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t+1}(s,a)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t+1}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t+1}(s,a)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t}(s,a)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C^{2}_{\phi}\left\|D_{t+1}-D_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\phi_{t}(s,a)\right\|_{2}+\left\|\phi_{t+1}(s,a)\right\|_{2}\right)\left\|\phi_{t}(s,a)-\phi_{t+1}(s,a)\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle C^{2}_{\phi}L_{\pi}\left\|\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\|_{2}+2C_{\phi}L_{\phi}\left\|\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(C^{2}_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2C_{\phi}L_{\phi}\right)\beta_{t}\left\|\phi^{\top}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)\beta_{t},

(56)

where
(a)
follows from
(
Zou et al., 2019
)
and Theorem 1 in
(
Li et al., 2021b
)
, where
L_{\pi}=\frac{1}{2}C_{\pi}\left(1+\left\lceil\log m^{-1}\right\rceil+\frac{1}{1-\rho}\right)
, and

\displaystyle\left\|D_{t+1}-D_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}\leq L_{\pi}\left\|\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\|_{2}.

(57)

We then consider the term
(i)
,

\displaystyle(i)

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|F_{t+1}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\right\|_{2}\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L_{J}\beta_{t}}{\lambda_{\min}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\right),

(58)

where the last inequality follows from
Lemma

1
.

Next, we consider the term
(ii)
,

\displaystyle(ii)

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{t}C^{2}_{\phi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|F_{t+1}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\right\|_{2}\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2BC^{2}_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}L_{J}m\rho^{k}\beta_{t}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}},

(59)

where the last inequality follows from
Lemma

1
and
Proposition

4
.

Then, consider the term
(iii)
,

\displaystyle(iii)

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{t}C^{2}_{\phi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|F_{t+1}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\right\|_{2}\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{2}_{\phi}L_{J}\beta_{t}}{\lambda_{\min}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right].

(60)

Consider the term
(iv)
,

\displaystyle(iv)=-\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle(F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t}))^{\top}(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}),{\phi_{t}^{\top}(s_{t},a_{t})\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}-\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s,a)\right]\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=-\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}[(F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t}))^{\top}(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})-(F_{t-k}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}))^{\top}(\theta_{t-k}-\theta^{*}_{t-k}),

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{\phi_{t}^{\top}(s_{t},a_{t})\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}-\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s,a)\right]\rangle]

\displaystyle\quad-\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle(F_{t-k}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}))^{\top}(\theta_{t-k}-\theta^{*}_{t-k}),{\phi_{t}^{\top}(s_{t},a_{t})\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}-\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s,a)\right]\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2C^{2}_{\phi}B\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|(F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t}))^{\top}(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})-(F_{t-k}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}))^{\top}(\theta_{t-k}-\theta^{*}_{t-k})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}[\langle(F_{t-k}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}))^{\top}(\theta_{t-k}-\theta^{*}_{t-k}),{\phi_{t}^{\top}(s_{t},a_{t})\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}-\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta_{t}\phi_{t}(s,a)\right]\rangle]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}2C^{2}_{\phi}B\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|(F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t}))^{\top}(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})-(F_{t-k}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}))^{\top}(\theta_{t-k}-\theta^{*}_{t-k})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2B^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(s_{t},a_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-k}\right)-D_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}2C^{2}_{\phi}B\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|(F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t}))^{\top}(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})-(F_{t-k}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}))^{\top}(\theta_{t-k}-\theta^{*}_{t-k})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2B^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}L_{J}\beta_{t}}{\lambda_{\min}}\left(C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{j}\right\|_{2}\right]+m\rho^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2C^{2}_{\phi}B\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|(F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t}))^{\top}(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})-(F_{t-k}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}))^{\top}(\theta_{t-k}-\theta^{*}_{t-k})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2B^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}L_{J}\beta_{t}}{\lambda_{\min}}\left(C_{\pi}C^{2}_{\phi}B\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}+m\rho^{k}\right),

(61)

where
(a)
follows from
Lemma

1
and
(b)
follows from
Lemma

4
.

Consider the first term in
61
and we have that

\displaystyle\left\|(F_{t+1}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t}))^{\top}(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})-(F_{t-k}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}))^{\top}(\theta_{t-k}-\theta^{*}_{t-k})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(F_{t+1}^{-1}-F_{t-k}^{-1}\right)\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t}))^{\top}(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})\right\|_{2}+\left\|F_{t-k}^{-1}\left(\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})-\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1})\right)(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|(F_{t-k}^{-1}\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}))^{\top}\left(\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-k}-\theta^{*}_{t}+\theta^{*}_{t-k}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2BL_{J}\left\|F_{t+1}^{-1}-F_{t-k}^{-1}\right\|_{2}+\frac{2B}{\lambda_{\min}}\left\|\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})-\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\left(\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-k}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t-k}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{2B^{2}L_{J}C^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\beta_{j}+\frac{2B}{\lambda_{\min}}\left\|\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})-\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\left(\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-k}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-k}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t-k}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-k}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{2B^{2}L_{J}C^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\beta_{j}+\frac{2BL_{\Theta}}{\lambda_{\min}}\left\|\hat{\omega}_{t}-\hat{\omega}_{t-k-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\left(\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-k}\right\|_{2}+C_{\Theta}\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{t-k}\right\|_{2}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2B^{2}L_{J}C^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\beta_{j}+\frac{2B^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}L_{\Theta}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\beta_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\left((k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}+BC^{2}_{\phi}C_{\Theta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}L_{J}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{BC^{2}_{\phi}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\left(2BC_{\phi}L_{J}(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi})+2BL_{\Theta}\lambda_{\min}+C_{\Theta}\lambda_{\min}\right)\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\beta_{j},

(62)

where
(a)
follows from
55
and
56
and
(b)
follows from
Lemma

7
.

Therefore, the term
(iv)
can be bounded as:

\displaystyle(iv)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2(k+1)BC^{3}_{\phi}U_{\delta}L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\beta_{t}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}

\displaystyle+\frac{2BC^{2}_{\phi}L_{J}\left(2C_{\text{gap}}+B\lambda_{\min}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}m\rho^{k}\beta_{t}+\frac{2B^{3}C_{\pi}C^{4}_{\phi}L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\beta_{t}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}

\displaystyle+\frac{2B^{2}C^{4}_{\phi}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\left(2BC_{\phi}L_{J}(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi})+2BL_{\Theta}\lambda_{\min}+C_{\Theta}\lambda_{\min}\right)\beta_{t}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\beta_{j}.

(63)

Combining the above bounds on terms
(i),(ii),(iii)
and
(iv)
,
we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]\leq\frac{BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)+\lambda_{\min}(L_{J}+2C_{\phi})+2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}{2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{2BC^{2}_{\phi}L_{J}\left(3C_{\text{gap}}+\lambda_{\min}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}m\rho^{k}\beta_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{2(k+1)BC^{3}_{\phi}U_{\delta}L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\beta_{t}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}+\frac{2B^{3}C_{\pi}C^{4}_{\phi}L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\beta_{t}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2B^{2}C^{4}_{\phi}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\left(2BC_{\phi}L_{J}(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi})+2BL_{\Theta}\lambda_{\min}+C_{\Theta}\lambda_{\min}\right)\beta_{t}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\beta_{j}.

(64)

This bounds term III in
48
.

Plugging bounds on terms I, II, III in
48
further implies that

\displaystyle E

\displaystyle\left[\left\|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\leq\left(1-\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha_{t}+\frac{BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)+\lambda_{\min}(L_{J}+2C_{\phi})+2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(k+1)^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}\alpha_{t}^{2}+\frac{4(k+1)BC^{3}_{\phi}U_{\delta}L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\beta_{t}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}+2(k+1)C^{3}_{\phi}BU_{\delta}C_{\Theta}\alpha_{t}\beta_{t}

\displaystyle+\left(2BC_{\phi}L_{J}(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi})+2BL_{\Theta}\lambda_{\min}+C_{\Theta}\lambda_{\min}\right)\frac{4B^{2}C^{4}_{\phi}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\beta_{j}+2C^{4}_{\phi}B^{2}C_{\Theta}^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4BC^{2}_{\phi}L_{J}\left(3C_{\text{gap}}+\lambda_{\min}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}m\rho^{k}\beta_{t}+\frac{4(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\alpha_{t}+\left(\frac{2}{\beta_{t}}+8\right)\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4B^{3}C_{\pi}C^{4}_{\phi}L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\beta_{t}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}+2\alpha_{t}G^{\delta}_{t}.

(65)

Set the stepsize such that
\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha_{t}}{2}\geq\frac{BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)+\lambda_{\min}(L_{J}+2C_{\phi})+2\lambda^{2}_{\min}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}
, and the proof ends.
∎

B.4
Sample Complexity of AC

We first present our proof of
Proposition

5
. For convenience, we set
\alpha_{t}=\alpha
,

\beta_{t}=\beta
,
\gamma_{t}=\gamma
in following.

Proposition 8
.

(Restatement of
Proposition

5
)
With the constant step sizes such that
k=\mathcal{O}\left(\log T\right)
and
\gamma\geq\alpha\geq\beta
, the tracking error of the AC algorithm in
Algorithm

1
can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{c_{\alpha}\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{c_{\eta}\beta}{\gamma}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{T\alpha}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log^{2}T}{T\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha\log^{3}T\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\beta\log^{3}T\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\gamma\log^{3}T\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\beta^{2}\log^{2}T}{\alpha}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\beta^{2}\log^{2}T}{\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}(m\rho^{k})\right)+\mathcal{O}\left((m\rho^{k})\log^{2}T\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(m\rho^{k})^{2}}{\alpha\beta}\right),

(66)

where
c_{\alpha}=\frac{2L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+2BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}
and
c_{\eta}=\frac{2B^{2}(k+1)^{2}C^{6}_{\phi}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}}
.

Proof.

Recall that in
Lemma

10
, we showed

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}\right]+G_{t}^{\theta}.

(67)

Apply this inequality recursively and we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}{2}\right)^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{0}-\theta^{*}_{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}{2}\right)^{t-j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{j})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}{2}\right)^{t-j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{j})-\eta_{j}\right)^{2}\right]+\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}{2}\right)^{t-j-1}G^{\theta}_{j}

\displaystyle=\left(1-q\right)^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{0}-\theta^{*}_{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-q\right)^{t-j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{j})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-q\right)^{t-j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{j})-\eta_{j}\right)^{2}\right]+\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-q\right)^{t-j-1}G^{\theta}_{j},

(68)

where we let
q=\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}{2}
.

Summing the inequality in
68
above w.r.t.
t
from
0
to
T-1
further implies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(1-q\right)^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{0}-\theta^{*}_{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\frac{\beta}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-q\right)^{t-j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{j})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-q\right)^{t-j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{j})-\eta_{j}\right)^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-q\right)^{t-j-1}G^{\theta}_{j}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4B^{2}}{Tq}+\frac{L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\frac{\beta}{Tq}\sum_{j=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{j})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\frac{\alpha}{Tq}\sum_{j=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{j})-\eta_{j}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{Tq}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G^{\theta}_{t},

(69)

where the last inequality is from the double-sum trick:
\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}\sum^{t-1}_{j=0}y^{t-j-1}X_{j}\leq(\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}X_{t})(\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}y^{t})\leq\frac{\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}X_{t}}{1-y}
for
X_{j}\geq 0,j=0,1,2,...,T-1
and
y\in(0,1)
.

Recall that we showed in
Lemma

9
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t+1}-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\left(1-\gamma\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}\right]+C_{\phi}^{4}B^{2}{\beta}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+G_{t}^{\eta}.

(70)

Recursively applying this inequality implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\gamma\right)^{t}\left(\eta_{0}-J(\omega_{0})\right)^{2}+C_{\phi}^{4}B^{2}\beta\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{t-j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{j})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{t-j-1}G_{j}^{\eta}

\displaystyle\leq R^{2}_{\max}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{t}+C_{\phi}^{4}B^{2}\beta\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{t-j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{j})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{t-j-1}G_{j}^{\eta}.

(71)

We then sum the above inequality w.r.t.
t
from 0 to
T-1
, and have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{R^{2}_{\max}}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{t}+C_{\phi}^{4}B^{2}\frac{\beta}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{t-j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{j})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{t-j-1}G_{j}^{\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{R^{2}_{\max}}{T\gamma}+C_{\phi}^{4}B^{2}\frac{\beta}{T\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{T\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\eta},

(72)

where we use the double-sum trick below
Equation

69
again.

Plugging
Equation

72
in
Equation

69
further implies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{8B^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}+\frac{2L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+2BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\frac{\beta}{T\alpha}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G^{\theta}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+2BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{2(k+1)^{2}B^{2}C^{6}_{\phi}\beta}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}\gamma}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{8B^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}+\frac{2}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G^{\theta}_{t}+\frac{2(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}R^{2}_{\max}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}T\gamma}+\frac{2(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}}\frac{1}{T\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\eta}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{c_{\alpha}\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{c_{\eta}\beta}{\gamma}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{8B^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}+\frac{2}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G^{\theta}_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}R^{2}_{\max}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}T\gamma}+\frac{2(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}}\frac{1}{T\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\eta},

(73)

where
(a)
follows from that
q=\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}{2}
,
c_{\alpha}=\frac{2L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+2BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}
and
c_{\eta}=\frac{2(k+1)^{2}B^{2}C^{6}_{\phi}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}}
.
By
73
, if we set the stepsize
\beta=\min\left\{\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\min}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}{16C^{4}_{\phi}\left(L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)\right)},\frac{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}\gamma}{16(k+1)^{2}C^{10}_{\phi}B^{2}}\right\}
, then we can get that
\left(\frac{c_{\alpha}\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{c_{\eta}\beta}{\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{1}{4C^{4}_{\phi}}
. This completes the proof of
Proposition

5
.
∎

We are now ready to prove
Theorem

1
.

Theorem 3
.

(Restatement of
Theorem

1
)
Consider the AC algorithm in
Algorithm

1
with constant step sizes
\alpha_{t}=\alpha,\beta_{t}=\beta,\gamma_{t}=\gamma
. Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\phi}^{4}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{T\beta}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\beta\log^{2}T\right)+\mathcal{O}(m\rho^{k}).

(74)

If we further set
k=\left\lceil\frac{\log T}{1-\rho}\right\rceil,\gamma=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}),\alpha=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}\log^{2}T}),\beta=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}\log^{2}T})
, then we have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{\log^{3}T}}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

(75)

Proof.

Recall
Equation

28
, and we have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{T})\right]-J(\omega_{0})\right)}{T\beta}+\frac{2C^{4}_{\phi}}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{T\beta}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\omega}.

(76)

Plug
Equation

76
in
73
, and we have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4C^{4}_{\phi}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{8B^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}+\frac{2}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G^{\theta}_{t}+\frac{2(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}R^{2}_{\max}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}T\gamma}+\frac{2(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}}\frac{1}{T\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2C^{4}_{\phi}T\beta}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{T})\right]-J(\omega_{0})\right)+\frac{1}{2T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{8B^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}+\frac{1}{2C^{4}_{\phi}T\beta}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G^{\omega}_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}}\left(\frac{R^{2}_{\max}}{T\gamma}+\frac{1}{T\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\eta}\right)+\frac{2}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G^{\theta}_{t}.

(77)

This further implies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\leq\frac{1}{C^{4}_{\phi}T\beta}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{T})\right]-J(\omega_{0})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{16B^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}+\frac{1}{C^{4}_{\phi}T\beta}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G^{\omega}_{t}+\frac{4(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}}\left(\frac{R^{2}_{\max}}{T\gamma}+\frac{1}{T\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\eta}\right)+\frac{4}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G^{\theta}_{t}.

(78)

Next, we choose the stepsizes to minimize the tracking error and the gradient norm.
We choose
\gamma=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}
and
k=\left\lceil\frac{\log T}{1-\rho}\right\rceil
. Consider
T\geq\frac{2C^{2}_{\phi}dm}{\lambda_{\min}}
, then we can get that
\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\geq\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{2}
.
We set
\alpha
and
\beta
such that
\alpha=\frac{1}{(k+1)^{2}}\gamma
and
\beta=\min\left\{\frac{\lambda^{3}_{\min}}{4\left(L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)+2\lambda^{2}_{\min}\right)}\alpha,\frac{\lambda^{3}_{\min}}{32C^{4}_{\phi}\left(L_{J}\lambda_{\min}+BC^{3}_{\phi}\left(C_{\phi}L_{\pi}+2L_{\phi}\right)\right)}\alpha,\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}{64(k+1)^{2}C^{6}_{\phi}B^{2}}\gamma\right\}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\gamma=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),\alpha=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}\log^{2}T}\right),\beta=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}\log^{2}T}\right),q=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}\log^{2}T}\right).

(79)

According to
29
,
35
and
46
, the orders of the following terms are as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\omega}=\mathcal{O}\left((m\rho^{k})\beta+k^{2}\beta^{2}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{T\log^{2}T}\right);

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\eta}=\mathcal{O}\left((m\rho^{k})\gamma+\beta^{2}+k^{2}\beta\gamma+k\gamma^{2}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log T}{T}\right);

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\theta}=\mathcal{O}\left(k(m\rho^{k})\alpha+(m\rho^{k})\beta+\frac{(m\rho^{k})^{2}}{\beta}+k^{3}\alpha^{2}+k^{3}\alpha\beta+k^{2}\beta^{2}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{T\log T}\right).

(80)

Then
78
can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log^{3}T}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

(81)

Plugging
78
in
Equation

76
implies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32C^{4}_{\phi}B^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}+\frac{8(k+1)^{2}C^{6}_{\phi}}{(\bar{\lambda}_{\min})^{2}}\left(\frac{R^{2}_{\max}}{T\gamma}+\frac{1}{T\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\eta}\right)+\frac{8C^{4}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}T\alpha}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\theta}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4}{T\beta}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{T})\right]-J(\omega_{0})\right)+\frac{4}{T\beta}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}G_{t}^{\omega}.

(82)

Plugging in the step-sizes above, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log^{3}T}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

(83)

which completes the proof.
∎

Appendix C
NAC Sample Complexity Analysis

In this section, we provide the sample complexity analysis for NAC.

C.1
Bound on Gradient Norm in NAC

Recall that in
Algorithm

1
, NAC updates the policy parameter as follows:

\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}=\beta_{t}\theta_{t}
, which directly implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\|_{2}\leq\beta_{t}\left\|\theta_{t}\right\|_{2}\leq B\beta_{t}.

(84)

Consider the largest eigenvalue of the matrix

F_{t}=\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t}(s,a)\right]
. Note that for any vector
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}

\displaystyle\left\|F_{t}x\right\|_{2}=\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t}(s,a)x\right]\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\phi_{t}(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|\phi_{t}(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|x\right\|_{2}\leq C^{2}_{\phi}\left\|x\right\|_{2}.

(85)

Thus,
\lambda_{\max}\left(F_{t}\right)\leq C^{2}_{\phi}
.
Then, by
Lemma

1
, we can show that

\displaystyle J

\displaystyle(\omega_{t+1})\geq J(\omega_{t})+\left\langle\nabla J(\omega_{t}),\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\rangle-\frac{L_{J}}{2}\left\|\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq J(\omega_{t})+\beta_{t}\left\langle\nabla J(\omega_{t}),\theta_{t}\right\rangle-\frac{\beta_{t}^{2}L_{J}}{2}\left\|\theta_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq J(\omega_{t})+\beta_{t}\left\langle\nabla J(\omega_{t}),\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\rangle+\beta_{t}\left\langle\nabla J(\omega_{t}),\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\rangle+\beta_{t}\left\langle\nabla J(\omega_{t}),\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\rangle-\frac{L_{J}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\geq}J(\omega_{t})+\beta_{t}\left\langle\nabla J(\omega_{t}),\left(\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t}(s,a)\right]\right)^{-1}\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\rangle-\frac{\beta_{t}}{4C^{2}_{\phi}}\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}-C^{2}_{\phi}\beta_{t}\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{\beta_{t}}{4C^{2}_{\phi}}\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}-C^{2}_{\phi}\beta_{t}\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{L_{J}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\geq}J(\omega_{t})+\frac{\beta_{t}}{C^{2}_{\phi}}\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{\beta_{t}}{2C^{2}_{\phi}}\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}-C^{2}_{\phi}\beta_{t}\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{C_{\text{gap}}C^{2}_{\phi}m\rho^{k}\beta_{t}}{\lambda_{\min}}-\frac{L_{J}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle=J(\omega_{t})+\frac{\beta_{t}}{2C^{2}_{\phi}}\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}-C^{2}_{\phi}\beta_{t}\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{C_{\text{gap}}C^{2}_{\phi}m\rho^{k}\beta_{t}}{\lambda_{\min}}-\frac{L_{J}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}}{2},

(86)

where
(a)
follows from that
\left\langle\nabla J(\omega_{t}),\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\rangle\geq-\frac{1}{4C^{2}_{\phi}}\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}-C^{2}_{\phi}\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}
and
(b)
follows from that
\lambda_{\max}(F_{t})\leq C^{2}_{\phi}
,
\left\langle\nabla J(\omega_{t}),\left(\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi_{t}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{t}(s,a)\right]\right)^{-1}\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\rangle=\nabla J(\omega_{t})^{\top}\left(F_{t}\right)^{-1}\nabla J(\omega_{t})\geq\frac{1}{C^{2}_{\phi}}\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2},
and
Proposition

4
.

Taking the expectation on both sides of
86
,
we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 2C^{2}_{\phi}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t+1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]}{\beta_{t}}+2C_{\phi}^{4}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{2C^{4}_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+{L_{J}B^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}\beta_{t}}.

(87)

C.2
Bound on
|\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})|
in NAC

In this section, we bound the term
\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})
for the NAC algorithm.

Lemma 11
.

If we denote

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}^{\eta}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\gamma_{t}\left(BR_{\max}^{2}C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}+R^{2}_{\max}m\rho^{k}+R_{\max}^{2}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\gamma_{j}+BC_{J}R_{\max}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}\right)

\displaystyle+R_{\max}^{2}\gamma^{2}_{t}+C_{J}^{2}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}+2BC_{J}R_{\max}\beta_{t}\gamma_{t}+R_{\max}L_{J}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2},

(88)

and set
\gamma_{t}-\gamma^{2}_{t}\geq\beta_{t}
,
then it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t+1}-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\left(1-\gamma_{t}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}\right]+{\beta_{t}}B^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\widetilde{G}^{\eta}_{t}.

(89)

Proof.

Similar to the AC analysis in
Equation

37
in
Section

B.2
, we have that

\displaystyle\left(\eta_{t+1}-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\left(1-\gamma_{t}\right)\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)+\gamma_{t}(R_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))+J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\gamma_{t}\right)^{2}\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}+\gamma_{t}^{2}\left(R_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}+\left(J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\gamma_{t}\underbrace{\left(R_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)\left(J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)}_{\text{I}}+2\gamma_{t}\left(1-\gamma_{t}\right)\underbrace{\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)\left(R_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)}_{\text{II}}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\left(1-\gamma_{t}\right)\underbrace{\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)\left(J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)}_{\text{III}}.

(90)

The term
\left|J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t+1})\right|
can be bounded using its Lipschitz smoothness in
Lemma

1
:

\displaystyle\left|J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t+1})\right|\leq C_{J}\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{t+1}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{J}B\beta_{t}.

(91)

Term I in
Equation

90
can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(R_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))(J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t+1}))\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|R_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right|\left|J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t+1})\right|\right]\leq BC_{J}R_{\max}\beta_{t}.

(92)

Term II in
Equation

90
can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))(R_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(\eta_{t-k}-J(\omega_{t-k}))(R_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))\right]\right|+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(\eta_{t}-\eta_{t-k}-J(\omega_{t})+J(\omega_{t-k}))(R_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[{(\eta_{t-k}-J(\omega_{t-k}))(R_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))}|\mathcal{F}_{t-k}\right]\right]\right|+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\eta_{t}-\eta_{t-k}-J(\omega_{t})+J(\omega_{t-k})\right|\left|R_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[{(\eta_{t-k}-J(\omega_{t-k}))R_{t}}|\mathcal{F}_{t-k}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim D_{t}}\left[{(\eta_{t-k}-J(\omega_{t-k}))R(s,a)}|\mathcal{F}_{t-k}\right]\right]\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\eta_{t}-\eta_{t-k}-J(\omega_{t})+J(\omega_{t-k})\right|\left|R_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}R^{2}_{\max}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{P}\left((s_{t},a_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t-k}\right),D_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}\right]+R_{\max}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\eta_{t}-\eta_{t-k}\right|+\left|J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t-k})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}R_{\max}^{2}\left(C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{j}\right\|_{2}\right]+m\rho^{k}\right)+R_{\max}\left(R_{\max}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\gamma_{j}+C_{J}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{t-k}\right\|_{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq BR_{\max}^{2}C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}+R^{2}_{\max}m\rho^{k}+R_{\max}^{2}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\gamma_{j}+R_{\max}C_{J}B\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\beta_{j},

(93)

where
(a)
follows from
\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim D_{t}}\left[R(s,a)-J(\omega_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]=0
,
(b)
follows from
0\leq\eta_{t}\leq R_{\max}
,
0\leq J(\omega_{t})\leq R_{\max}
,
0\leq R_{t}\leq R_{\max}
, and

(c)
follows from
Lemma

4
.

We then bound term III as follows:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)\left(J(\omega_{t})-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\qquad=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))\left(-\nabla^{\top}J(\omega_{t})(\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t})+\left(\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right)^{\top}\frac{\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})}{2}\left(\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right)\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))\nabla^{\top}J(\omega_{t})(\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))\left(\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right)^{\top}\frac{\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})}{2}\left(\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\qquad=\beta_{t}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t}))\nabla^{\top}J(\omega_{t})\theta_{t}\right]\right|+\beta^{2}_{t}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right|\frac{\left\|\nabla^{2}J(\hat{\omega}_{t})\right\|_{2}}{2}\left\|\theta_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{\beta_{t}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}\right]+\frac{B^{2}\beta_{t}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{R_{\max}L_{J}B^{2}}{2}\beta_{t}^{2},

(94)

where the first equation is from the Lagrange’s Mean Value theorem for some
\hat{\omega}_{t}=\lambda\omega_{t}+(1-\lambda)\omega_{t+1}
,
\lambda\in(0,1)
.

Plug
Equation

92
,
Equation

93
, and
94
in
Equation

90
, and we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left(\eta_{t+1}-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\left((1-\gamma_{t})^{2}+{\beta_{t}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}{\beta_{t}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{t}\left(BR_{\max}^{2}C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}+R^{2}_{\max}m\rho^{k}+R_{\max}^{2}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\gamma_{j}+R_{\max}C_{J}B\sum_{j=t-k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}\right)

\displaystyle+R_{\max}^{2}\gamma^{2}_{t}+C_{J}^{2}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}+2BC_{J}R_{\max}\beta_{t}\gamma_{t}+R_{\max}L_{J}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}.

(95)

In
95
, we use the fact that
1-\gamma_{t}\leq 1
.
This completes the proof.
∎

C.3
Tracking Error Analysis of NAC

In this section, we bound the tracking error
\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}
for NAC. Define

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}^{\theta}_{t}=

\displaystyle\left(8+\frac{2}{\beta_{t}}+\beta_{t}C_{\Theta}\right)\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}+(k+1)^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}C_{\phi}^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}+\frac{4(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\alpha_{t}

\displaystyle+2(k+1)C_{\phi}BU_{\delta}C_{\Theta}\alpha_{t}\beta_{t}+2\alpha_{t}G_{t}^{\delta}+2C_{\Theta}^{2}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}.

(96)

Lemma 12
.

If we set the step size satisfies that
\beta_{t}\leq\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}{4(2C_{\Theta}+1)}\alpha_{t}
, then it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha_{t}}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{\Theta}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\theta}.

(97)

Proof.

From the update rule of
Algorithm

1
, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\Pi_{B}\left(\theta_{t}+\alpha_{t}\delta_{t}z_{t}\right)-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\left\|\theta_{t}+\alpha_{t}\delta_{t}z_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\theta_{t}+\alpha_{t}\delta_{t}z_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}+\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\alpha_{t}^{2}\left\|\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\alpha_{t}\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+2\alpha_{t}\left\langle\delta_{t}z_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle+2\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle,

(98)

where
(a)
follows from the fact
\left\|\Pi_{B}(x)-y\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|x-y\right\|_{2}
when
\left\|y\right\|_{2}\leq B
and
\left\|\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}\leq B
.

Taking expectations on both sides of
98
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left\|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\alpha_{t}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+2\alpha_{t}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\rangle\right]}_{\text{I}}+2\alpha_{t}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\delta_{t}z_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]}_{\text{II}}+2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]}_{\text{III}}.

(99)

For the term
\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}+\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}+\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t+1}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}C_{\Theta}\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{t+1}\right\|_{2}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle=\beta_{t}C_{\Theta}\left\|\theta_{t}\right\|_{2}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\Theta}B\beta_{t}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}},

(100)

where
(a)
follows from
Lemma

2
and
Proposition

4
. Hence we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 2C^{2}_{\Theta}B^{2}\beta^{2}_{t}+\frac{8C^{2}_{\text{gap}}m^{2}\rho^{2k}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}.

(101)

By Lemma
5
, term I in
99
can be bounded as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\rangle\right]\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{2\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]+G^{\delta}_{t}.

(102)

For term II in
99
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\delta_{t}z_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t+1}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}C_{\Theta}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]+\frac{2(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle\leq(k+1)C_{\phi}BU_{\delta}C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}+\frac{2(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}},

(103)

where
(a)
follows from
Lemma

2
and
Proposition

4
.

For term III in
99
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\rangle\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}C_{\Theta}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]+\frac{\beta_{t}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2\beta_{t}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\beta_{t}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2\beta_{t}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta^{*}_{t+1}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}C_{\Theta}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\theta_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]+C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2}C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2}C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{2}C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\frac{1}{2}C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2}C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left(F_{t}\right)^{-1}\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{C_{\Theta}}{2}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{C_{\Theta}\beta_{t}}{2}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}+\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}\left(2C_{\Theta}+1\right)\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{C_{\Theta}}{2\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\left(\frac{\beta_{t}C_{\Theta}}{2}+\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\right)\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2},

(104)

where
(a)
follows from
Lemma

2
;
(b)
follows from
Proposition

4
;
(c)
follows from
Proposition

1
,
Proposition

2
, and
Proposition

4
;
(d)
follows from that
\left\|F_{t}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}}
.

Plugging the above bounds in
99
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left\|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha_{t}+\left(4C_{\Theta}+2\right)\beta_{t}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{C_{\Theta}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]+\left(8+\frac{2}{\beta_{t}}+\beta_{t}C_{\Theta}\right)\left(\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}+(k+1)^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}C_{\phi}^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\alpha_{t}+2(k+1)C_{\phi}BU_{\delta}C_{\Theta}\alpha_{t}\beta_{t}+2\alpha_{t}G_{t}^{\delta}+2C_{\Theta}^{2}B^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}.

(105)

This hence completes the proof.
∎

C.4
Sample Complexity of NAC

In the following, we set
\alpha_{t}=\alpha
,
\beta_{t}=\beta
, and
\gamma_{t}=\gamma
for any
t\geq 0
. We denote by
\text{KL}(\omega_{t})=-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\log\frac{\pi_{\omega_{t}}(a|s)}{\pi^{*}(a|s)}\right]
. Recall that
\hat{t}=\lceil\frac{3\log T}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}\rceil
. We first have the following lemma.

Lemma 13
.

Denote by
\widetilde{T}={\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}\hat{t}\geq\frac{T}{\log T}
, then for any
t^{\prime}\leq T-\widetilde{T}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\min_{t\leq T}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[J(\pi^{*})-J(\omega_{t})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+2C_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+\sqrt{2}C_{\phi}\left(\frac{2eC_{\phi}^{3}C_{M}\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\right)+\sqrt{2}C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{2}C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)}+2C_{\phi}\sqrt{C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{e\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}}

\displaystyle+2C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{e\hat{t}C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta\right)}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta+\frac{2R_{\max}}{\sqrt{T}}.

(106)

Proof.

Recall that
\pi^{*}=\arg\max_{\pi}J(\pi)
and
A^{\pi_{t}}=Q^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)-V^{\pi_{t}}(s)
. We first have that

KL

\displaystyle(\omega_{t})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t+1})=\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\log\pi_{t+1}(a|s)-\log\pi_{t}(a|s)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\geq}\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\nabla^{\top}\log\pi_{t}(a|s)\right]\left(\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right)-\frac{L_{\phi}}{2}\left\|\omega_{t+1}-\omega_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\beta\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta_{t}\right]-\frac{L_{\phi}\beta^{2}}{2}\left\|\theta_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\beta\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[A^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)\right]+\beta\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta^{*}_{t}-A^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)\right]+\beta\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})\right]-\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}\beta^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}\beta\left(J(\pi^{*})-J(\pi_{t})\right)+\beta\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta^{*}_{t}-A^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)\right]}_{\text{I}}+\beta\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})\right]}_{\text{II}}-\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}\beta^{2}}{2},

(107)

where
(a)
follows from that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}[\log\pi_{\omega}(a|s)]-\nabla\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}[\log\pi_{\omega^{\prime}}(a|s)]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}[\phi_{\omega}(s,a)-\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}(s,a)]\right\|_{2}\leq\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}[L_{\phi}\left\|\omega-\omega^{\prime}\right\|_{2}]=L_{\phi}\left\|\omega-\omega^{\prime}\right\|_{2},

(108)

and
(b)
follows from the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[A^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[Q^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)-V^{\pi_{t}}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[R(s,a)-J(\pi_{t})+V^{\pi_{t}}(s^{\prime})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[R(s,a)\right]-J(\pi_{t})+\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim D_{\pi^{*}},s\sim P(\cdot|s,a)}\left[V^{\pi_{t}}(s^{\prime})\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[V^{\pi_{t}}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=J(\pi^{*})-J(\pi_{t})+\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[V^{\pi_{t}}(s^{\prime})\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[V^{\pi_{t}}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=J(\pi^{*})-J(\pi_{t}).

(109)

To bound Term I, we first have that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta^{*}_{t}-A^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-A^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)\right]\right|+\left|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)(\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{D_{\pi^{*}}}{D_{t}}\right\|_{\infty}\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\left|\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-A^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)\right|\right]+C_{\phi}\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\left\|\frac{D_{\pi^{*}}}{D_{t}}\right\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\left(\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-A^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)\right)^{2}\right]}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\left\|\frac{D_{\pi^{*}}}{D_{t}}\right\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}},

(110)

where
(a)
follows from that
proposition

4
,
and
(b)
follows from the definition of
\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}
in
Equation

2
, the definition of
\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}
and the facts that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\left(\phi^{\top}_{t}(s,a)\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-A^{\pi_{t}}(s,a)\right)^{2}\right]\leq\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}.

(111)

For term II, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t})\right]\right|\leq C_{\phi}\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}.

(112)

Plug the two bounds on terms I and II in
Equation

107
, and we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\text{KL}(\omega_{t})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\text{KL}(\omega_{t+1})\right]\geq

\displaystyle\beta\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)-\beta\left\|\frac{D_{\pi^{*}}}{D_{t}}\right\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}-\beta\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle-\beta C_{\phi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]-\frac{L_{\phi}}{2}\beta^{2}B^{2},

(113)

which implies

\displaystyle\beta\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\text{KL}(\omega_{t})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\text{KL}(\omega_{t+1})\right]+\beta\left\|\frac{D_{\pi^{*}}}{D_{t}}\right\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+\beta\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle+\beta C_{\phi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta^{2}.

(114)

Set
M_{t+1}=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t+1}-J(\omega_{t+1})\right)^{2}\right]
, and we now aim to bound
M_{t}
. Combine the bounds we obtained in
89
and
97
, and we have that

\displaystyle M_{t+1}\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{C_{\Theta}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\beta\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(1-\gamma\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t}-J(\omega_{t})\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\beta\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\eta}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]+\left(1-\gamma\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{C_{\Theta}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}+B^{2}\right)\beta\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\eta}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]+\left(1-\gamma\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{C_{\Theta}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}+B^{2}\right)\beta\left(\frac{2C_{\phi}^{2}}{\beta}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t+1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)+2C^{4}_{\phi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{C_{\Theta}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}+B^{2}\right)\left(2C_{\text{gap}}\frac{C_{\phi}^{4}m\rho^{k}\beta}{\lambda_{\min}}+{L_{J}C^{2}_{\phi}B^{2}\beta^{2}}\right)+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\eta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha+2C_{\phi}^{4}C_{M}\beta\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+{2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t+1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(1-\gamma+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]+C_{M}\left(\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}C_{\phi}^{4}m\rho^{k}\beta}{\lambda_{\min}}+{L_{J}C^{2}_{\phi}B^{2}\beta^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\eta},

(115)

where
(a)
is obtained by
87
, and
C_{M}=\frac{C_{\Theta}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}+B^{2}
.
For convenience, we set

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{t}=C_{M}\left(\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}C_{\phi}^{4}m\rho^{k}\beta}{\lambda_{\min}}+{L_{J}C^{2}_{\phi}B^{2}\beta^{2}}\right).

(116)

We set
k=\lceil\frac{\log T}{1-\rho}\rceil
and
T\geq\frac{2C^{2}_{\phi}dm}{\lambda_{\min}}
such that
\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\geq\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{\min}
. Furthermore, we set the step sizes such that
\frac{1}{6}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha\geq 2C_{\phi}^{4}C_{M}\beta
and
\frac{\gamma}{2}\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{3}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha,\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\alpha\right\}
. Let
q=\frac{1}{3}\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha
and
\frac{\gamma}{2}\geq q
. Then the inequality in
115
can be written as

\displaystyle M_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-q\right)M_{t}+{2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t+1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)+\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{t}+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\eta}.

(117)

Recall that
\hat{t}=\left\lceil\frac{1}{q}\log T\right\rceil=\left\lceil\frac{3\log T}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}\right\rceil
.
For
t\geq 2k+\hat{t}
, we recursively apply
Equation

117
for
\hat{t}
times, and we have that

\displaystyle M_{t}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-q\right)^{\hat{t}}M_{t-\hat{t}}+2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{j+1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{j})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{T}+2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{j+1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{j})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right),

(118)

where
(a)
follows from
(1-q)^{\hat{t}}\leq e^{-q\hat{t}}\leq e^{-\log T}=\frac{1}{T}
and
M_{i}=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{i}-\theta_{i}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{i}-J(\omega_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq 4B^{2}+R^{2}_{\max}
for
i=0,1,...,T
.

Denote the time length
\widetilde{T}={\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}\hat{t}\geq\frac{T}{\log T}
. For any
t^{\prime}\leq T-\widetilde{T}
, together with
Equation

114
we have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\left\|\frac{D_{\pi^{*}}}{D_{t}}\right\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C_{\phi}\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}\right]+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\left\|\frac{D_{\pi^{*}}}{D_{t}}\right\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+C_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}M_{t}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta,

(119)

where
(a)
follows from the rearrangement inequality and the fact for any random variable
X
,
\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[X\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|X\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]
and
(b)
follows from Assumption
3
.

Moreover, for
2k+\hat{t}\leq t^{\prime}\leq T-\widetilde{T}
, summing
118
w.r.t.
t
from
t^{\prime}
to
t^{\prime}+\tilde{T}-1
implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}M_{t}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{j+1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{j})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{i=0}^{\hat{t}-1}\sum_{t=\hat{t}-i}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}(1-q)^{i}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t-i})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t-i-1})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{i=0}^{\hat{t}-1}(1-q)^{i}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-i-1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t^{\prime}-i-1})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{i=0}^{\hat{t}-1}(1-q)^{i}\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t^{\prime}-i-1})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{i=0}^{\hat{t}-1}\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t^{\prime}-i-1})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right),

(120)

where
(a)
follows from that we set
i=t-j-1
,
(b)

follows from the fact that
\sum_{t=\hat{t}-i}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-i-1}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t-i})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t-i-1})\right]\right)=\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-i-1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t^{\prime}-i-1})\right]
and
(c)
follows from
J(\pi^{*})\geq J(\pi_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-i-1})
.

Lemma 14
.

We denote by
X_{t^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{\hat{t}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}-\hat{t}}^{t^{\prime}-1}\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)
and
Y_{t^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)
.
We have that
there must exist
t^{\prime}+\hat{t}\leq t^{\prime\prime}\leq t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}
s.t.

X_{t^{\prime\prime}}\leq Y_{t^{\prime}}
.

Proof.

We prove the lemma by contraction. Assume that there does not exist
t^{\prime}+\hat{t}\leq t^{\prime\prime}\leq t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}
s.t.

X_{t^{\prime\prime}}\leq Y_{t^{\prime}}
. Then, for any
t^{\prime}+\hat{t}\leq t^{\prime\prime}\leq t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}
,
X_{t^{\prime\prime}}>Y_{t^{\prime}}
.

Next, recall
\widetilde{T}={\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}\hat{t}
, then we have that

\displaystyle Y_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)=\frac{1}{\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}\sum_{\tau=1}^{\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}\frac{1}{\hat{t}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}+(\tau-1)\hat{t}}^{t^{\prime}+\tau\hat{t}-1}\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}\sum_{\tau=1}^{\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}X_{t^{\prime}+\tau\hat{t}}\overset{(a)}{>}\frac{1}{\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}\sum_{\tau=1}^{\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}Y_{t^{\prime}}=Y_{t^{\prime}},

(121)

where
(a)
follows from
t^{\prime}+\hat{t}\leq t^{\prime}+\tau\hat{t}\leq t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}
,
X_{t+\tau\hat{t}}>Y_{t^{\prime}}
for
\tau=1,...,\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil
. This hence results in a contradiction, which completes the proof.
∎

We then discuss the following two cases.

Case 1:

[For any
2k+\hat{t}\leq t^{\prime}\leq T-\widetilde{T}
, it holds that
eY_{t^{\prime}}<X_{t^{\prime}}
.]

Then, for
\widetilde{t}_{0}=2k+\hat{t}
, we have

X_{\widetilde{t}_{0}}>eY_{\widetilde{t}_{0}}
. Recall that
\widetilde{T}={\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}\hat{t}\leq\left(\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}+1\right)\hat{t}\leq\frac{T}{\log T}+\hat{t}\leq\frac{2T}{\log T}
for large
T
. Then,
\frac{T}{\widetilde{T}}\geq\frac{\log T}{2}\geq\lfloor\frac{\log T}{2}\rfloor
.
Thus, there exist
\widetilde{t}_{0}+\hat{t}\leq\widetilde{t}_{1}\leq\widetilde{t}_{0}+\widetilde{T}
, s.t.

X_{\widetilde{t}_{1}}\overset{(a)}{\leq}Y_{\widetilde{t}_{0}}\overset{(b)}{<}\frac{1}{e}X_{\widetilde{t}_{0}}
, where
(a)
follows from
Lemma

14
and
(b)
follows from the condition of case 1.
Recursively applying the above argument, we have that for
j=0,1,...,\lfloor\frac{\log T}{2}\rfloor
, there exists
\widetilde{t}_{j}+\hat{t}\leq\widetilde{t}_{j+1}\leq\widetilde{t}_{j}+\widetilde{T}
, such that
X_{\widetilde{t}_{j+1}}\leq Y_{\widetilde{t}_{j}}<\frac{1}{e}X_{\widetilde{t}_{j}}
. This further implies that

\displaystyle X_{\widetilde{t}_{0}}>eY_{\widetilde{t}_{0}}\geq eX_{\widetilde{t}_{1}}>e^{2}Y_{\widetilde{t}_{1}}\geq...\geq e^{j}Y_{\widetilde{t}_{j}}>e^{j+1}Y_{\widetilde{t}_{j}}\geq...\geq e^{{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}}X_{\widetilde{t}_{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}}>e^{{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}+1}Y_{\widetilde{t}_{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}}.

(122)

Then, by
122
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle Y_{\widetilde{t}_{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}}:=\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=\widetilde{t}_{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}}^{\widetilde{t}_{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}+\widetilde{T}-1}\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)\leq\frac{1}{e^{{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}+1}}X_{\widetilde{t}_{0}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}X_{\widetilde{t}_{0}}.

(123)

Note that
X_{\widetilde{t}}\leq J(\pi^{*})\leq R_{\max}
, hence we have that

\displaystyle Y_{\widetilde{t}_{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}}=\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=\widetilde{t}_{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}}^{\widetilde{t}_{\lfloor{\frac{\log T}{2}}\rfloor}+\widetilde{T}-1}\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]\right)\leq\frac{R_{\max}}{\sqrt{T}}.

(124)

This further implies that

\displaystyle\min_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[J(\pi^{*})-J(\omega_{t})\right]\leq\frac{R_{\max}}{\sqrt{T}}.

(125)

This hence completes the proof of
Theorem

2
under Case 1.

Case 2:

[There exists some
2k+\hat{t}\leq t^{\prime}\leq T-\widetilde{T}
s.t.
X_{t^{\prime}}\leq eY_{t^{\prime}}
.]

Define
Z_{t^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}M_{t}
. From
Equation

120
, we obtain that

\displaystyle Z_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}M_{t}\leq 2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{i=0}^{\hat{t}-1}\left(J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t^{\prime}-i-1})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)

\displaystyle=2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}X_{t^{\prime}}+\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{e\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}Y_{t^{\prime}}+\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right),

(126)

where
(a)
follows from the condition of Case 2.

Next, from
119
, we have that

\displaystyle Y_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}J(\pi^{*})-\mathbb{E}\left[J(\omega_{t})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+C_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}M_{t}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta

\displaystyle=\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+C_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+C_{\phi}\sqrt{Z_{t^{\prime}}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta.

(127)

Plug
127
in
126
, and we have that

\displaystyle Z_{t^{\prime}}
