\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}M_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)+\frac{2eC_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta\right)

\displaystyle\quad+2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{e\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+C_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+C_{\phi}\sqrt{Z_{t^{\prime}}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)+\frac{2eC_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{2eC_{\phi}^{3}C_{M}\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{2}Z_{t^{\prime}}+\frac{C^{2}_{\infty}\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}{2C^{2}_{\phi}}+2C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{e\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}\right),

(128)

where
(a)
follows from
xy\leq\frac{x^{2}+y^{2}}{2}

Thus, it follows that

\displaystyle Z_{t^{\prime}}\leq\frac{8B^{2}+2R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}+\frac{2}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)+2\left(\frac{2eC_{\phi}^{3}C_{M}\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\right)^{2}+\frac{C^{2}_{\infty}\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}{C^{2}_{\phi}}

\displaystyle\quad+4C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{e\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}\right)+\frac{4eC_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta\right).

(129)

Then, we get that

\displaystyle Y_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+C_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}M_{t}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+2C_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+C_{\phi}\sqrt{2}\left(\frac{2eC_{\phi}^{3}C_{M}\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\right)

\displaystyle+C_{\phi}\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)}+C_{\phi}\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}}

\displaystyle+2C_{\phi}\sqrt{C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{e\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}}+2C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{e\hat{t}C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta\right)}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta,

(130)

which proves the claim under Case 2.

Thus, combine the Case 1 result in
124
and the Case 2 result in
130
, we have that

\displaystyle\min_{t\leq T}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[J(\pi^{*})-J(\omega_{t})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+2C_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+C_{\phi}\sqrt{2}\left(\frac{2eC_{\phi}^{3}C_{M}\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\right)+C_{\phi}\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}}

\displaystyle+C_{\phi}\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)}+2C_{\phi}\sqrt{C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{e\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}}

\displaystyle+2C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{e\hat{t}C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta\right)}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta+\frac{2R_{\max}}{\sqrt{T}}.

(131)

which completes the proof.
∎

Next, we prove
Theorem

2
.

Theorem 4
.

(Restatement of
Theorem

2
)
Consider the NAC algorithm in
Algorithm

1
with constant step sizes that
\gamma\geq\alpha\geq\beta
, then it holds that

\displaystyle\min_{t\leq T}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[J(\pi^{*})-J(\omega_{t})\right]\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log^{2}T}{T\alpha}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log T}{T\beta}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log^{3}T}}{T\sqrt{\alpha\beta}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\beta\log T}{T\alpha}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log T}{T}}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\gamma\beta\log^{2}T}}{\sqrt{\alpha}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\gamma\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{\alpha}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\beta}{\sqrt{\alpha}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\alpha\log^{3}T}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\beta\log^{3}T}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{(m\rho^{k})\log T}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{(m\rho^{k})\beta}{\alpha}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{(m\rho^{k})\gamma}{\alpha}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m\rho^{k}}{\sqrt{\alpha\beta}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}\right).

If we set
\gamma=\mathcal{O}(T^{-\frac{2}{3}}\log T),\alpha=\mathcal{O}(T^{-\frac{2}{3}}\log^{-1}T),\beta=\mathcal{O}(T^{-\frac{2}{3}}\log^{-1}T)
, we have

\displaystyle\min_{t\leq T}J(\pi^{*})-J(\omega_{t})\leq\mathcal{O}\left({T^{-\frac{1}{3}}}\log^{3}T\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}\right).

(132)

Proof.

From
Lemma

13
, we have that

\displaystyle\min_{t\leq T}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[J(\pi^{*})-J(\omega_{t})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}+\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+2C_{\infty}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}+C_{\phi}\sqrt{2}\left(\frac{2eC_{\phi}^{3}C_{M}\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\right)+C_{\phi}\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{4B^{2}+R_{\max}^{2}}{\widetilde{T}}}

\displaystyle+C_{\phi}\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)}+2C_{\phi}\sqrt{C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}\frac{e\hat{t}}{\widetilde{T}}\frac{\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}})-\text{KL}(\omega_{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}})}{\beta\widetilde{T}}}

\displaystyle+2C_{\phi}\sqrt{\frac{e\hat{t}C_{\phi}^{2}C_{M}}{\widetilde{T}}\left(\frac{C_{\phi}C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta\right)}+\frac{B^{2}L_{\phi}}{2}\beta+\frac{2R_{\max}}{\sqrt{T}}.

(133)

We then set the stepsize as follows:

\displaystyle\gamma

\displaystyle=T^{-\frac{2}{3}}\log T;

\displaystyle\alpha

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}\gamma,\frac{3}{2\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\right\}=\mathcal{O}\left(T^{-\frac{2}{3}}\log^{-1}T\right);

\displaystyle\beta

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}{4(2C_{\Theta}+1)}\alpha,\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}{12C_{\phi}^{4}C_{M}}\alpha\right\}=\mathcal{O}\left(T^{-\frac{2}{3}}\log^{-1}T\right).

(134)

Recall that

\displaystyle k=\lceil\frac{\log T}{1-\rho}\rceil;

\displaystyle q=\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\alpha}{2}=\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha\right);

\displaystyle\hat{t}=\left\lceil\frac{1}{q}\log T\right\rceil=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log T}{\alpha}\right);

\displaystyle\widetilde{T}={\left\lceil\frac{T}{\hat{t}\log T}\right\rceil}\hat{t}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{T}{\log T}\right).

(135)

Applying the above stepsizes in
134
, for
t\leq T
, we can have that

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}^{\eta}_{t}=\mathcal{O}\left((m\rho^{k})\gamma+\beta^{2}+k^{2}\beta\gamma+k\gamma^{2}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(T^{-\frac{4}{3}}\log^{3}T\right);

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\theta}=\mathcal{O}\left(k(m\rho^{k})\alpha+\frac{(m\rho^{k})^{2}}{\beta}+k^{3}\alpha^{2}+k^{3}\alpha\beta+\beta^{2}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(T^{-\frac{4}{3}}\log T\right);

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}_{t}^{\omega}=\mathcal{O}\left((m\rho^{k})\beta+\beta^{2}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(T^{-\frac{4}{3}}\log^{-2}T\right).

(136)

Besides, we have that
\text{KL}(\omega_{i})=\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\log\left(\frac{\pi^{*}(a|s)}{\pi_{\omega_{i}}(a|s)}\right)\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi^{*}}}\left[\log\left(\frac{D_{\pi^{*}}(s,a)}{D_{\pi_{\omega_{i}}}(s,a)}\right)\right]\overset{(a)}{\leq}\log C_{\infty}
,
where
(a)
follows from Assumption
3
.

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{\widetilde{T}}\sum_{t=t^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}+\widetilde{T}-1}\sum_{j=t-\hat{t}}^{t-1}(1-q)^{t-j-1}\left(\widetilde{G}^{\omega}_{j}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\theta}+\widetilde{G}_{j}^{\eta}\right)}=\mathcal{O}\left(T^{-\frac{1}{3}}\log^{2}T\right).

(137)

Plugging the above equations to
131

, we have that

\displaystyle\min_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[J(\pi^{*})-J(\omega_{t})\right]\leq\left(T^{-\frac{1}{3}}\log^{3}T\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\text{actor}}}\right).

(138)

This concludes the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Proof of Lemmas

Proof of Lemma
1
.

Recall the definition of
\nabla J(\omega)
in
Equation

1
:

\displaystyle\nabla J(\omega)=\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[Q^{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)\phi_{\omega}(s,a)\right],

(139)

which implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla J(\omega)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[Q^{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)\phi_{\omega}(s,a)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi^{\top}_{\omega}(s,a)\bar{\theta}^{*}_{\omega}\phi_{\omega}(s,a)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\phi}^{2}\left(\left\|\theta^{*}_{\omega}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{\omega}-\theta^{*}_{\omega}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}C^{2}_{\phi}\left(B+C_{\text{gap}}\frac{m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)=C_{J},

(140)

where
(a)
follows from
3
and
(b)
follows from
Proposition

4
.
It hence proves the first claim.
The second claim is proved in Lemma A.1 in
(
Wu et al., 2020
)
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
2
.

Recall that
F_{\omega}=\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{\omega}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{\omega}(s,a)\right]
. From the definition of
\bar{\theta}^{*}_{\omega}
in
Equation

2
, it can be verified that

\displaystyle\bar{\theta}^{*}_{\omega}=F_{\omega}^{-1}\nabla J(\omega).

(141)

Hence

\displaystyle\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{\omega}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{\omega^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\left\|F_{\omega}^{-1}\nabla J(\omega)-F_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-1}\nabla J({\omega^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left\|F_{\omega}^{-1}\nabla J(\omega)-F_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-1}\nabla J({\omega})\right\|_{2}+\left\|F_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-1}\nabla J({\omega})-F_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-1}\nabla J({\omega^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{(a)}{\leq}\left\|F_{\omega}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|F_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|F_{\omega}-F_{\omega^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\left\|\nabla J(\omega)\right\|_{2}+\left\|(F_{\omega^{\prime}})^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|\nabla J({\omega})-\nabla J({\omega^{\prime}})\right\|_{2},

(142)

where
(a)
follows from
55
.
Note that
F_{\omega}
can be shown to be Lipschitz as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|F_{\omega}-F_{\omega^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{{\omega}}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{{\omega}}(s,a)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\left[\phi_{{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{{\omega}}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{{\omega}}(s,a)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\left[\phi_{{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\phi^{\top}_{{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\phi}L_{\phi}\left\|\omega^{\prime}-\omega\right\|_{2}+C^{2}_{\phi}\left\|D_{\pi_{\omega}}-D_{\pi_{\omega^{\prime}}}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}2C_{\phi}L_{\phi}\left\|\omega^{\prime}-\omega\right\|_{2}+C^{2}_{\phi}L_{\pi}\left\|\omega-\omega^{\prime}\right\|_{2},

(143)

where
(a)
follows from
(
Zou et al., 2019
)
and Theorem 1 in
(
Li et al., 2021b
)
, that

\displaystyle\left\|D_{\pi_{\omega}}-D_{\pi_{\omega^{\prime}}}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}\leq L_{\pi}\left\|\omega-\omega^{\prime}\right\|_{2}.

(144)

Hence combining
142
,
143
and Lemma
1
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{\omega}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{\omega^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{C_{J}}{\lambda^{2}_{\min}}\left(2C_{\phi}L_{\phi}+C^{2}_{\phi}L_{\pi}\right)+\frac{L_{J}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)\left\|\omega-\omega^{\prime}\right\|_{2}.

(145)

This completes the proof.

∎

Proof of Lemma
3
.

From the definition of
H_{\omega}
in
11
, we first have that

\displaystyle H_{\omega}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{\omega}(s_{0},a_{0})\left(\phi_{\omega}(s_{k},a_{k})-\phi_{\omega}(s_{0},a_{0})\right)^{\top}|s_{0}=s,a_{0}=a,\pi_{\omega}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{\omega}(s,a)\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{\omega}^{\top}(s_{k},a_{k})|s_{0}=s,a_{0}=a,\pi_{\omega}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{\omega}^{\top}(s,a)\right]\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{\omega}(s,a)\phi_{\omega}^{\top}(s,a)\right],

(146)

where
(a)
follows from
\phi_{\omega}(s,a)=\nabla\log\pi_{\omega}(a|s)
and
\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{\omega}^{\top}(s,a)f(s)\right]=0
, where
f(s)
is the function which is not determined by action
a
.

Define
\Delta H_{\omega}=\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{\omega}(s,a)\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{\omega}^{\top}(s_{k},a_{k})|s_{0}=s,a_{0}=a,\pi_{\omega}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{\omega}^{\top}(s,a)\right]\right)\right]
. Thus,

\displaystyle\frac{H_{\omega}+H_{\omega}^{\top}}{2}=\frac{\Delta H_{\omega}+\Delta H_{\omega}^{\top}}{2}-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{\omega}(s,a)\phi_{\omega}^{\top}(s,a)\right].

(147)

For any symmetric matrices
X
and
Y
,
\lambda_{\max}\left(X+Y\right)\leq\lambda_{\max}\left(X\right)+\lambda_{\max}\left(Y\right)
.
Thus, we have that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{H_{\omega}+H_{\omega}^{\top}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{\Delta H_{\omega}+\Delta H_{\omega}^{\top}}{2}\right)+\lambda_{\max}\left(-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[\phi_{\omega}(s,a)\phi_{\omega}^{\top}(s,a)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\phi}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|P(s_{k},a_{k}|s_{0}=s,a_{0}=s,\pi_{\omega}),D_{\pi_{\omega}}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}\right]-\lambda_{\min}

\displaystyle\leq dC_{\phi}^{2}m\rho^{k}-\lambda_{\min}=-\bar{\lambda}_{\min},

(148)

where last inequality follows from Assumption
1
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
4
.

Conditioned on
(s_{t-k},a_{t-k})
, the sample trajectory in
Algorithm

1
is generated according to the following Markov chain:

\displaystyle(s_{t-k},a_{t-k})\xrightarrow{\pi_{t-k}\times{P}}(s_{t-k+1},a_{t-k+1})\xrightarrow{\pi_{t-k+1}\times{P}}...(s_{t},a_{t})\xrightarrow{\pi_{t}\times{P}}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1}).

(149)

Using the technique in
(
Zou et al., 2019
)
, we construct an auxiliary Markov chain as follows. Before time
t-k
, the states and actions are generated according to
Algorithm

1
; and after time
t-k
, all the subsequent state-action pairs, denoted by
(\widetilde{s}_{l},\widetilde{a}_{l})
, are generated according to a fixed policy
\pi_{t}
and transition kernel
P
:

\displaystyle(s_{t-k},a_{t-k})\xrightarrow{\pi_{t}\times{P}}(\widetilde{s}_{t-k+1},\widetilde{a}_{t-k+1})\xrightarrow{\pi_{t}\times{P}}...(\widetilde{s}_{t},\widetilde{a}_{t})\xrightarrow{\pi_{t}\times{P}}(\widetilde{s}_{t+1},\widetilde{a}_{t+1}).

(150)

Denote by
\widetilde{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
the filtration corresponding to the auxiliary Markov chain designed in
150
.

Then follow steps similar to those in
(
Zou et al., 2019
, Appendix B)
and
(
Li et al., 2021b
, Lemma 6)
, it can be shown that

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(s_{t},a_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-k}\right)-D_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}}

\displaystyle\leq m\rho^{k}+\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}C_{\pi}\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{j}\right\|_{2}.

(151)

∎

Proof of Lemma
5
.

Define the sum of the feature along the trajectory as follows:

\displaystyle z_{t}=\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\phi_{j}(s_{j},a_{j}),\quad\hat{z}_{t}=\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\phi_{t}(s_{j},a_{j})\text{ and }\widetilde{z_{t}}=\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\phi_{t}(\widetilde{s}_{j},\widetilde{a}_{j}).

(152)

For every policy
\pi_{t}
, we construct another auxiliary Markov chain, denoted by
\{\left(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j}\right)\}_{j=0}^{\infty}
, which is under the stationary distribution induced by policy
\pi_{t}
and transition kernel
P
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\left(\bar{s}_{0},\bar{a}_{0}\right)\sim D_{t},

(153)

and all the subsequent actions are generated by
\pi_{t}
. Define

\displaystyle\bar{z}_{t}=\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\phi_{t}(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j}).

(154)

Denote by
\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})=R(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t})-J(\omega_{t})+\phi_{t}^{\top}(\bar{s}_{t+1},\bar{a}_{t+1})\theta_{t}-\phi_{t}^{\top}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t})\theta_{t}
.

Lemma 15
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta,\omega_{t})|\pi_{t}\right]=H_{\omega_{t}}\theta+b_{\omega_{t}}.

(155)

From the definition in
Equation

4
,
\theta^{*}_{t}
is the fixed point of the
k
-step TD operator
\mathcal{T}_{\pi_{t}}^{(k)}
. Then, it follows that

\displaystyle H_{\omega_{t}}\theta^{*}_{t}+b_{\omega_{t}}=\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi^{\top}_{t}(s,a)\left(\mathcal{T}_{\pi_{t}}^{(k)}\left(\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta^{*}_{t}\right)-\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta^{*}_{t}\right)\right]=\mathbf{0}.

(156)

Together with Lemma
15
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta^{*}_{t},\omega_{t})\right]=0.

(157)

Thus we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta^{*}_{t},\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},H_{\omega_{t}}\left(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right)\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{H_{\omega_{t}}+H_{\omega_{t}}^{\top}}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}-\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right],

(158)

where
(a)
follows from
Lemma

3
.

Then, recall
\hat{z}_{t}=\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\phi_{t}(s_{j},a_{j})
. Denote by
\hat{\delta}_{t}=R(s_{t},a_{t})-J(\omega_{t})+\phi^{\top}_{t}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})\theta_{t}-\phi^{\top}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\theta_{t}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},z_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},z_{t}\delta_{t}-\hat{z}_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}-\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},z_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},z_{t}\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq-\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},z_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{2\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right],

(159)

where
(a)
follows from
Equation

158
.

Consider the term

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},z_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]
, and we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},z_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t}+\theta^{*}_{t-2k},z_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k},z_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|z_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\hat{\delta}_{t}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\bar{z}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})\right\|_{2}\right)\right]}_{(i)}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k},z_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\hat{z}_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}\right\rangle\right]}_{(ii)}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k},\hat{z}_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t},\omega_{t})\right\rangle\right]}_{(iii)}.

(160)

In AC algorithm, recall
U_{\delta}=R_{\max}+2C_{\phi}B
. Then, consider
\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}
and
\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}\delta_{j}z_{j}\right\|_{2}\leq\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}\left|\delta_{j}\right|\left\|z_{j}\right\|_{2}\overset{(a)}{\leq}(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j},

(161)

where
(a)
follows from the fact that
\left\|z_{t}\right\|_{2}\leq(k+1)C_{\phi}
.

Then, it can be shown that

\displaystyle\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-2k}+\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}+\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\Theta}\left\|{\omega_{t}-\omega_{t-2k}}\right\|_{2}+\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\Theta}\left\|\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}\phi_{j}^{\top}(s_{j},a_{j})\theta_{j}\phi_{j}(s_{j},a_{j})\right\|_{2}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\Theta}C^{2}_{\phi}B\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}.

(162)

Thus, from
162
, the term
(i)
in
Equation

160
can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle(i)\leq 2(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\left((k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}+C_{\Theta}C^{2}_{\phi}B\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}+2C_{\text{gap}}\frac{m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right).

(163)

Then, for term
(ii)
in
Equation

160
, it can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle(ii)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}\left\|z_{t}-\hat{z}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left|\delta_{t}(\theta_{t})\right|\right]\leq 2BU_{\delta}\left\|\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\phi_{j}(s_{j},a_{j})-\phi_{t}(s_{j},a_{j})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2BU_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}C_{\pi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{j}\right\|_{2}\right]\leq 2BU_{\delta}C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\sum_{i=j}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\beta_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}(s_{i},a_{i})\theta_{i}\phi_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2B^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}U_{\delta}C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\sum_{i=j}^{t}\beta_{i}.

(164)

Next, for term
(iii)
in
Equation

160
, we can show that

\displaystyle(iii)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k},\hat{z}_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k},\hat{z}_{t}\hat{\delta}_{t}-\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta_{t})\right\rangle|\mathcal{F}_{t-2k}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4BC_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(s_{j},a_{j}|\mathcal{F}_{t-2k}\right)-D_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4BC_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(s_{j},a_{j}|\mathcal{F}_{t-2k}\right)-D_{j}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}+\left\|D_{j}-D_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4BC_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\left(C_{\pi}\sum_{i=j-k}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}\right\|_{2}\right]+m\rho^{k}+L_{\pi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{j}\right\|_{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4BC_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\left(C_{\pi}\sum_{i=j-k}^{j-1}\sum_{\iota=i}^{j-1}\beta_{\iota}C^{2}_{\phi}B+m\rho^{k}+L_{\pi}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}C^{2}_{\phi}B\right).

(165)

Thus, combining
163
,
Equation

164
and
Equation

165
, we can bound term as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\rangle\right]\leq-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{2\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+2B^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}U_{\delta}C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\sum_{i=j}^{t}\beta_{i}+4BC_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\left(BC^{2}_{\phi}C_{\pi}\sum_{i=j-k}^{j-1}\sum_{\iota=i}^{j-1}\beta_{\iota}+m\rho^{k}+BC^{2}_{\phi}L_{\pi}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}\right)

\displaystyle+2(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\left((k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}+C_{\Theta}C^{2}_{\phi}B\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right).

(166)

In NAC algorithm,
terms
\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}
and
\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}
can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}\delta_{j}z_{j}\right\|_{2}\leq(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j},

(167)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-2k}+\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}+\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\theta^{*}_{t}-\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\bar{\theta}^{*}_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\Theta}\left\|\left(\omega_{t}-\omega_{t-2k}\right)\right\|_{2}+\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\Theta}\left\|\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}\theta_{j}\right\|_{2}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\Theta}B\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}.

(168)

Thus, using
168
and
167
, term
(i)
in
Equation

160
can be bounded as

\displaystyle(i)\leq 2(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\left((k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}+C_{\Theta}B\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}+2C_{\text{gap}}\frac{m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right).

(169)

Next, we bound the term
(ii)
in
Equation

160
as follows:

\displaystyle(ii)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k}\right\|_{2}\left\|z_{t}-\hat{z}_{t}\right\|_{2}\left|\hat{\delta}_{t}\right|\right]\leq 2BU_{\delta}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\phi_{j}(s_{j},a_{j})-\phi_{t}(s_{j},a_{j})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2BU_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}C_{\pi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{j}\right\|_{2}\right]\leq 2BU_{\delta}C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\left\|\beta_{i}\theta_{i}\right\|_{2}\leq 2B^{2}U_{\delta}C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}.

(170)

Term
(iii)
in
Equation

160
can be bounded as

\displaystyle(iii)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k},\hat{z}_{t}\delta_{t}(\theta_{t})-z^{\prime}_{t}\delta^{\prime}_{t}(\theta_{t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\theta_{t-2k}-\theta^{*}_{t-2k},\hat{z}_{t}\delta_{t}(\theta_{t})-z^{\prime}_{t}\delta^{\prime}_{t}(\theta_{t})\right\rangle|\mathcal{F}_{t-2k}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2B2C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(s_{j},a_{j}|\mathcal{F}_{t-2k}\right)-D_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4BC_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(s_{j},a_{j}|\mathcal{F}_{t-2k}\right)-D_{j}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}+\left\|D_{j}-D_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4BC_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\left(C_{\pi}\sum_{i=j-k}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}\right\|_{2}\right]+m\rho^{k}+L_{\pi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\omega_{t}-\omega_{j}\right\|_{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4B^{2}C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\left(C_{\pi}\sum_{i=j-k}^{j-1}\sum_{\iota=i}^{j-1}\beta_{\iota}+m\rho^{k}+L_{\pi}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}\right).

(171)

Combining the above bounds on terms
(i),(ii),(iii)
, term can be bounded as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t},\delta_{t}z_{t}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\frac{(k+1)^{2}C^{2}_{\phi}}{2\bar{\lambda}_{\min}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(J(\omega_{t})-\eta_{t}\right)^{2}\right]+2B^{2}U_{\delta}C_{\pi}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}

\displaystyle+2(k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\left((k+1)C_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\alpha_{j}+C_{\Theta}B\sum_{j=t-2k}^{t-1}\beta_{j}+\frac{2C_{\text{gap}}m\rho^{k}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)

\displaystyle+4BC_{\phi}U_{\delta}\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\left(BC_{\pi}\sum_{i=j-k}^{j-1}\sum_{\iota=i}^{j-1}\beta_{\iota}+m\rho^{k}+BL_{\pi}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\beta_{i}\right).

(172)

This completes the proof.
∎

Proof of Lemma
7
.

From
\nabla^{2}J(\omega)=\sum_{s,a}\nabla^{2}D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)R(s,a)
, we can get that for any
\omega,\omega^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}J(\omega)-\nabla^{2}J(\omega^{\prime})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{s,a}\left(\nabla^{2}D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)R(s,a)-\nabla^{2}D_{\pi_{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)R(s,a)\right)\right\|_{2}.

(173)

Denote by
\bm{\sigma}(A)
the spectral radius of matrix
A\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
.
Recall the fact that

\bm{\sigma}(A)\leq\left\|A\right\|_{\infty}=\max_{i}\left\{\sum_{j}\left|a_{ij}\right|\right\}.

If
A
is symmetric matrix,
\left\|A\right\|_{2}=\bm{\sigma}(A)
, and thus

\left\|A\right\|_{2}\leq\max_{i}\left\{\sum_{j}\left|a_{ij}\right|\right\}.

It is clear that
\nabla^{2}D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)
is symmetric, and therefore
\sum_{s,a}(\nabla^{2}D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)-\nabla^{2}D_{\pi_{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a))R(s,a)
is also symmetric. It then follows that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{s,a}\left(\nabla^{2}D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)-\nabla^{2}D_{\pi_{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\right)R(s,a)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i}\left\{\sum_{j}\left|\sum_{s,a}\left(\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)-\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}D_{\pi_{\omega^{\prime}}}(s,a)\right)R(s,a)\right|\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\max_{i}\left\{\sum_{j}\left|\left(\nabla_{\omega}\sum_{s,a}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}(s,a)R(s,a)\right)^{\top}(\omega-\omega^{\prime})\right|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i}\left\{\sum_{j}\left\|\nabla_{\omega}\sum_{s,a}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}(s,a)R(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|\omega-\omega^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\max_{i}\left\{\sum_{j}\sum_{l}\left|\sum_{s,a}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{l}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}(s,a)R(s,a)\right|\left\|\omega-\omega^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j,l}\left\{d^{2}\left|\sum_{s,a}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{l}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}(s,a)R(s,a)\right|\right\}\left\|\omega-\omega^{\prime}\right\|_{2},

(174)

where
(a)
follows from the fact that
D_{\pi_{\omega}}
is
n
times differentiable as long as the Theorem 4 in
(
Heidergott & Hordijk, 2003
)
and the Lagrange’s Mean Value Theorem for some
\hat{\omega}=\lambda_{ij}\omega+(1-\lambda_{ij})\omega^{\prime}
with
\lambda_{ij}\in[0,1]
, and
(b)
follows from that for a vector
a
,
\left\|a\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|a\right\|_{1}
.

Define a function
v:\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\to\mathbb{R}
, denote by
\left\|f\right\|_{v}=\sup_{s,a}\frac{\left|f(s,a)\right|}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}
the finite
v
-norm of function
f
. Set
v(s,a)=e^{R(s,a)}
and we can get that

\displaystyle\sup_{s,a}\frac{\left|R(s,a)\right|}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}\leq 1.

(175)

Moreover,
v(s,a)=e^{R(s,a)}\leq e^{R_{\max}}
and
v(s,a)\geq 1
. If
\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1
, it implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{s,a}|f(s,a)|\leq e^{R_{\max}}.

(176)

For a (signed) measure
\mu
, the associated norm is

\displaystyle\left\|\mu\right\|_{v}=\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\left|\sum_{s,a}\mu(s,a)f(s,a)\right|.

(177)

For a kernel
P(s^{\prime},a^{\prime}|s,a)
, its associated norm is

\displaystyle\left\|P\right\|_{v}=\sup_{s,a}\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\frac{\left|\sum_{s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}f(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})P(s^{\prime},a^{\prime}|s,a)\right|}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}.

(178)

From the fact that
\sup_{s,a}\frac{\left|R(s,a)\right|}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}\leq 1
, we can get that
||R||_{v}\leq 1
. This further implies that

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{s,a}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{l}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}(s,a)R(s,a)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\left|\sum_{s,a}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{l}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}(s,a)f(s,a)\right|

\displaystyle=\left\|\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{l}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}.

(179)

Furthermore, we define
\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}(s,a)
,
\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{i,j}}(s,a)
and
\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{\omega_{i,j,l}}(s,a)
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}(s,a|s^{\prime},a^{\prime})=\sum_{\iota=0}^{\infty}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\pi_{\omega}(a|s)\left(\mathbb{P}\left(s_{\iota}=s,a_{\iota}=a|s_{0}=s^{\prime},a_{0}=a^{\prime},\pi_{\omega}\right)-D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)\right);

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{i,j}}(s,a|s^{\prime},a^{\prime})=\sum_{\iota=0}^{\infty}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\pi_{\omega}(a|s)\left(\mathbb{P}\left(s_{\iota}=s,a_{\iota}=a|s_{0}=s^{\prime},a_{0}=a^{\prime},\pi_{\omega}\right)-D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)\right);

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{\omega_{i,j,l}}(s,a|s^{\prime},a^{\prime})=\sum_{\iota=0}^{\infty}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{l}}\pi_{\omega}(a|s)\left(\mathbb{P}\left(s_{\iota}=s,a_{\iota}=a|s_{0}=s^{\prime},a_{0}=a^{\prime},\pi_{\omega}\right)-D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)\right).

(180)

Then, define the kernel
\Gamma_{\omega}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime}|s,a)
, s.t.,

\Gamma_{\omega}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime}|s,a)=D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})
for any
s\in\mathcal{S}
,
a\in\mathcal{A}
. It then follows that

\displaystyle\left\|\Gamma_{\omega}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle=\sup_{s,a}\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\frac{\left|\sum_{s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}f(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\Gamma_{\omega}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime}|s,a)\right|}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}

\displaystyle=\sup_{s,a}\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\frac{\left|\sum_{s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}f(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right|}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}=\sup_{s,a}\frac{\left\|D_{\pi_{\omega}}\right\|_{v}}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}.

(181)

By Theorem 3 and proof in
(
Heidergott & Hordijk, 2003
)
,
we can get

\displaystyle\partial_{\omega_{j}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}=\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}+\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}};

\displaystyle\partial_{\omega_{l}}\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}=\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{l}}+\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{\omega_{ijl}}.

(182)

Combining with Theorem 4 and Section 4 in
(
Heidergott & Hordijk, 2003
)
,
we can have

\displaystyle\partial_{\omega_{i}}\Gamma_{\omega}

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}};

\displaystyle\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\Gamma_{\omega}

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{\omega_{j}}\Gamma_{\omega}\right)\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}+\Gamma_{\omega}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}=\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}+\Gamma_{\omega}\left(\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}+\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}\right)

\displaystyle=2\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}+\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}};

\displaystyle\partial_{\omega_{l}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\Gamma_{\omega}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\omega_{l}}\left(2\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}+\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}\right)

\displaystyle=2\left(\partial_{\omega_{l}}\Gamma_{\omega}\right)\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}+2\Gamma_{\omega}\left(\partial_{\omega_{l}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\right)\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}+2\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\left(\partial_{\omega_{l}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\partial_{\omega_{l}}\Gamma_{\omega}\right)\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}+\Gamma_{\omega}\left(\partial_{\omega_{l}}\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}\right)

\displaystyle=2\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{l}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}+2\Gamma_{\omega}\left(\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{l}}+\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{il}}\right)\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}+2\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\left(\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{l}}+\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{jl}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{l}}\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}+\Gamma_{\omega}\left(\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{l}}+\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{\omega_{ijl}}\right)

\displaystyle=6\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{l}}+2\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{jl}}+2\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{j}}\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{il}}+2\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{l}}\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}+\Gamma_{\omega}\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{\omega_{ijl}}.

(183)

Then, according to the discussion in Section 4 in
(
Heidergott & Hordijk, 2003
)
, it can be shown that

\displaystyle\sup_{s,a}\frac{\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}

\displaystyle=\left\|\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{l}}\Gamma_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Gamma_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{ijl}}\right\|_{v}+2\left\|\Gamma_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{ij}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{l}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\|\Gamma_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{il}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{j}}\right\|_{v}+2\left\|\Gamma_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{lj}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{i}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle\quad+6\left\|\Gamma_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{i}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{j}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{l}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle=\sup_{s,a}\frac{1}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}\big(\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{ijl}}\right\|_{v}+2\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{ij}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{l}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{il}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{j}}\right\|_{v}+2\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{lj}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{i}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle\quad+6\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{i}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{j}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{l}}\right\|_{v}\big).

(184)

Note that
R_{\max}\geq R(s,a)\geq 0
and
\sup_{s,a}\frac{1}{\left|v(s,a)\right|}\geq e^{-R_{\max}}
. Then we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{l}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{ijl}}\right\|_{v}+2\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{ij}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{l}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{il}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{j}}\right\|_{v}+2\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{lj}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{i}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle\quad+6\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{i}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{{\hat{\omega}}_{j}}\right\|_{v}\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{(\hat{\omega})_{l}}\right\|_{v}.

(185)

Next, we bound the term
\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\left|\sum_{s,a}f(s,a)D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}(s,a)\right|\leq\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\left|\sum_{s,a}|f(s,a)|\left|D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}(s,a)\right|\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\left|\sum_{s,a}e^{R_{\max}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}(s,a)\right|\leq e^{R_{\max}},

(186)

where
(a)
follows from the
176
.

Then, we bound the terms in
Equation

185
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{(\hat{\omega})_{i}}\right\|_{v}

\displaystyle=\sup_{s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\frac{\left|\sum_{\iota=0}^{\infty}\sum_{s,a}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\pi_{\omega}(a|s)\left(\mathbb{P}\left(s_{\iota}=s,a_{\iota}=a|s_{0}=s^{\prime},a_{0}=a^{\prime},\pi_{\omega}\right)-D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)\right)f(s,a)\right|}{\left|v(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right|}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sup_{s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\left|\sum_{\iota=0}^{\infty}\sum_{s,a}\partial_{\omega_{i}}\pi_{\omega}(a|s)\left(\mathbb{P}\left(s_{\iota}=s,a_{\iota}=a|s_{0}=s^{\prime},a_{0}=a^{\prime},\pi_{\omega}\right)-D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)\right)f(s,a)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\sup_{\left\|f\right\|_{v}\leq 1}\left|\sum_{\iota=0}^{\infty}\sum_{s,a}\left|\partial_{\omega_{i}}\pi_{\omega}(a|s)\right|\left|\mathbb{P}\left(s_{\iota}=s,a_{\iota}=a|s_{0}=s^{\prime},a_{0}=a^{\prime},\pi_{\omega}\right)-D_{\pi_{\omega}}(s,a)\right||f(s,a)|\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sup_{s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\sum_{\iota=0}^{\infty}\max_{s,a}\left\{\left|\partial_{\omega_{i}}\pi_{\omega}(a|s)\right|\right\}\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(s_{\iota},a_{\iota}|s_{0}=s^{\prime},a_{0}=a^{\prime},\pi_{\omega}\right)-D_{\pi_{\omega}}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}e^{R_{\max}}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\max_{s,a}\left\{\left|\partial_{\omega_{i}}\pi_{\omega}(a|s)\right|\right\}\frac{me^{R_{\max}}}{1-\rho},

(187)

where
(a)
follows from that
\left|v(s,a)\right|\geq 1
for all
s\in\mathcal{S},a\in\mathcal{A}
,
(b)
follows from
176
and
(c)
follows from Assumption
1
.

Similar to
187
, by Assumption
1
and Assumption
2
, we can further bound
\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\right\|_{v}
,
\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}\right\|_{v}

and
\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{\omega_{ijl}}\right\|_{v}
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\omega_{i}}\right\|_{v}\leq\frac{mC_{\phi}e^{R_{\max}}}{1-\rho};\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\omega_{ij}}\right\|_{v}\leq\frac{mC_{\delta}e^{R_{\max}}}{1-\rho};\left\|\mathcal{K}^{(3)}_{\omega_{ijl}}\right\|_{v}\leq\frac{mL_{\delta}e^{R_{\max}}}{1-\rho}.

(188)

Plug
186
and
188
in
Equation

185
, and we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{\omega_{i}}\partial_{\omega_{j}}\partial_{\omega_{l}}D_{\pi_{\hat{\omega}}}\right\|_{v}\leq\frac{6C^{3}_{\phi}m^{3}e^{4R_{\max}}}{(1-\rho)^{3}}+\frac{6m^{2}C_{\phi}C_{\delta}e^{3R_{\max}}}{(1-\rho)^{2}}+\frac{mL_{\delta}e^{2R_{\max}}}{1-\rho}.

(189)

Thus, we can further get that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}J(\omega)-\nabla^{2}J(\omega^{\prime})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq d^{2}\left(\frac{6C^{3}_{\phi}m^{3}e^{4R_{\max}}}{(1-\rho)^{3}}+\frac{6m^{2}C_{\phi}C_{\delta}e^{3R_{\max}}}{(1-\rho)^{2}}+\frac{mL_{\delta}e^{2R_{\max}}}{1-\rho}\right)\left\|\omega-\omega^{\prime}\right\|_{2}.

(190)

∎

Proof of Lemma
15
.

Consider the probability
\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j},\bar{s}_{t-k},\bar{a}_{t-k}\right)

and term

\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{z}_{t}\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta,\omega_{t})|\pi_{t}\right]
.
We have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\phi_{t}^{\top}(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j})\left(R(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t})-J(\omega_{t})+\phi^{\top}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t+1},\bar{a}_{t+1})\theta-\phi_{t}^{\top}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t})\theta\right)\Big|\pi_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\phi_{t}^{\top}(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j})\bar{\delta}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta,\omega_{t})\Big|\pi_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{s,a}\left[\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j},\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t}\right)\phi^{\top}_{t}(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j})\delta_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t};\theta,\omega_{t})\Big|\pi_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{s,a}\left[\sum_{j=t-k}^{t}\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t},\bar{s}_{2t-j},\bar{a}_{2t-j}\right)\phi^{\top}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t})\delta_{t}(\bar{s}_{2t-j},\bar{a}_{2t-j};\theta,\omega_{t})\Big|\pi_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\sum_{s,a}\left[\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t},\bar{s}_{t+i},\bar{a}_{t+i})\right)\phi^{\top}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t})\delta_{t}(\bar{s}_{t+i},\bar{a}_{t+i};\theta,\omega_{t})\Big|\pi_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t})\sim D_{t}}\left[\phi^{\top}_{t}(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t})\sum_{i=0}^{k}\delta_{t}(\bar{s}_{t+i},\bar{a}_{t+i};\theta,\omega_{t})\Big|\pi_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{D_{t}}\left[\phi^{\top}_{t}(s,a)\left(\mathcal{T}^{(k)}\left(\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta\right)-\phi_{t}^{\top}(s,a)\theta\right)\right]

\displaystyle=H_{\omega_{t}}\theta+b_{\omega_{t}},

(191)

where
(a)
follows from the fact that
\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j}\right)=\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t}\right)\sim D_{t}
, and thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j},\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t}|\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j}\right)\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{j},\bar{a}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{2t-j},\bar{a}_{2t-j}|\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t}\right)\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t}\right)=\mathbb{P}\left(\bar{s}_{t},\bar{a}_{t},\bar{s}_{2t-j},\bar{a}_{2t-j}\right).

(192)

∎

Proof of
Lemma

6
.

Recall
b_{\omega}=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{k}\phi^{\top}_{\omega}(s_{0},a_{0})(R(s_{j},a_{j})-J(\omega))|(s_{0},a_{0})\sim D_{\pi_{\omega}},\pi_{\omega}\right]
. Recall the definition of
H_{\omega}
in
11
. Then, the solution to
Equation

4
can be written as

\displaystyle\theta^{*}_{\omega}=-A^{-1}_{\omega}b_{\omega}.

(193)

First,
b_{\omega}
can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|b_{\omega}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{k}\phi^{\top}_{\omega}(s_{0},a_{0})(R(s_{j},a_{j})-J(\omega))|(s_{0},a_{0})\sim D_{\pi_{\omega}},\pi_{\omega}\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\sum_{j=0}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi^{\top}_{\omega}(s_{0},a_{0})(R(s_{j},a_{j})-J(\omega))|(s_{0},a_{0})\sim D_{\pi_{\omega}},\pi_{\omega}\right]}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\sum_{j=0}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi^{\top}_{\omega}(s_{0},a_{0})\left(R(s_{j},a_{j})-\mathbb{E}_{D_{\pi_{\omega}}}\left[R(s,a)\right]\right)|(s_{0},a_{0})\sim D_{\pi_{\omega}},\pi_{\omega}\right]}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{\sum_{j=0}^{k}C_{\phi}R_{\max}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|D_{\pi_{\omega}}-\mathbb{P}\left(s_{j},a_{j}|s_{0},a_{0},\pi_{\omega}\right)\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}|(s_{0},a_{0})\sim D_{\pi_{\omega}}\right]}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}C_{\phi}R_{\max}\sum_{j=0}^{k}m\rho^{k}\leq\frac{C_{\phi}R_{\max}m}{1-\rho},

(194)

where
(a)
follows from the triangular inequality and the fact that for any probability distribution
P_{1}
and
P_{2}
, and any random variable
X
, s.t.

\left|X\right|\leq X_{\max}
,
\left|\mathbb{E}_{P_{1}}\left[X\right]-\mathbb{E}_{P_{2}}\left[X\right]\right|\leq X_{\max}\left\|P_{1}-P_{2}\right\|_{\mathcal{TV}}
,
(b)
follows from Assumption
1
.

From the following equation:

\displaystyle\theta^{*\top}_{\omega}H_{\omega}\theta^{*}_{\omega}=\theta^{*\top}_{\omega}b_{\omega}=\left(\theta^{*\top}_{\omega}b_{\omega}\right)^{\top}=\theta^{*\top}_{\omega}A^{\top}_{\omega}\theta^{*}_{\omega},

(195)

it holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{H_{\omega}+H_{\omega}^{\top}}{2}\right)\left\|\theta^{*}_{\omega}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\theta^{*\top}_{\omega}\frac{H_{\omega}+A^{\top}_{\omega}}{2}\theta^{*}_{\omega}=\theta^{*\top}_{\omega}b_{\omega}\geq-\left\|\theta_{\omega}^{*}\right\|_{2}\left\|b_{\omega}\right\|_{2}.

(196)

Thus, we can bound
\theta^{*}_{\omega}
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\theta^{*}_{\omega}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{H_{\omega}+H_{\omega}^{\top}}{2}\right)}\left\|b_{\omega}\right\|_{2}\overset{(b)}{\leq}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{H_{\omega}+H_{\omega}^{\top}}{2}\right)}\frac{mC_{\phi}R_{\max}}{1-\rho}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}-dC_{\phi}^{2}m\rho^{k}}\frac{mC_{\phi}R_{\max}}{1-\rho}=\frac{mC_{\phi}R_{\max}}{\bar{\lambda}_{\min}\left(1-\rho\right)},

(197)

where
(a)
follows from
196
,
(b)
follows from
194
and
(c)
follows from
Lemma

3
.
∎

Appendix E
Experiments

In this section, we conduct experiments to numerically verify our AC/NAC with compatible function
approximation. We test our algorithms in the Acrobot environment
(
Sutton, 1995
)
. The environment involves a two-link linear chain with one end anchored and a joint that can be actuated. The goal is to apply torques at this joint to swing the unanchored end of the chain to a certain height from an initial position of hanging down. We parameterize our policy using a neural network and use compatible function approximation in the critic part. We compare the performance between our AC/NAC with compatible function approximation and the standard AC/NAC with linear function approximation.

We first compare the performance of AC algorithms. We run vanilla AC,
1
-step AC with compatible function approximation, and
k
-step AC with compatible function approximation (shortened as AC, 1-step CAC and
k
-step CAC); And then we compare three NAC algorithms: vanilla NAC,
1
-step NAC with compatible function approximation and
k
-step NAC with compatible function approximation (shortened as NAC, 1-step CNAC and
k
-step CNAC).

In our experiment setup, we set
k=128
, and design a 2-layer neural network with 16 hidden neurons to represent the policy, which contains 163 parameters. We run the algorithms for 20 times. At each time step, after we obtain the policies from the algorithms, we evaluate them and plot the average reward in
Figure

2
and
Figure

2
among 20 runs.
We also plot the 90 and 10 percentiles of the 20 curves as the upper and lower envelopes of the curves.

Figure 1:
Vanilla AC with fixed feature function v.s. One-step AC with compatible feature function v.s.
128
-step AC with compatible feature function.

Figure 2:
Vanilla NAC with fixed feature function v.s. One-step NAC with compatible feature function v.s.
128
-step NAC with compatible feature function.

We observe that for both AC and NAC, our algorithms with compatible function approximation lead to better performances. As our theoretical results showed, this performance improvement is due to the fact that the compatible function approximation can avoid critic error, whereas the linear function approximation results in an inaccurate estimation of the value functions in the critic part.
